{{American film list}}

This is a list of American films released in [[2024 in film|2024]].

The year featured a diverse array of cinematic productions, ranging from major studio blockbusters to independent and streaming platform releases. The [[2023 Hollywood labor disputes]], including the [[2023 Writers Guild of America strike|Writers Guild of America strike]] and [[2023 SAG-AFTRA strike|SAG-AFTRA strike]], had a significant impact on the 2024 release schedule, with many films being postponed due to productions being halted mid-filming or before commencement.

Following the box office section, this list is organized chronologically, providing information on release dates, production companies, directors, and principal cast members.

== Box office ==
The highest-grossing American films released in 2024, by domestic box office [[gross revenue]], are as follows:<ref>{{Cite web |title=Domestic Box Office For 2024 |url=https://www.boxofficemojo.com/year/2024/?grossesOption=totalGrosses&sort=grossToDate&ref_=bo_yld__resort#table |access-date=April 17, 2025 |website=[[Box Office Mojo]]}}</ref><ref>{{Cite web |title=Top 2024 Movies at the Domestic Box Office |url=https://www.the-numbers.com/box-office-records/domestic/all-movies/cumulative/released-in-2024 |access-date=2025-04-17 |website=The Numbers}}</ref>
